SELECT `w`.`wishlist_id` AS `WishlistId`, `p`.`product_id` AS `ProductId`, `p`.`product_name` AS `ProductName`, `w`.`sku_id` AS `SkuId`, `p`.`buscat_id` AS `Buscatid`, `p`.`prod_subcat_id` AS `prod_sub_catid`, COALESCE(`p1`.`file_name`, `p3`.`file_name`) AS `VariantImageUrl`, COALESCE(`s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`sku_cost`) AS `Price`, `b`.`bus_reg_id` AS `StoreId`, `b`.`business_name` AS `StoreName`, (`p`.`product_status` = 'Approved') AND `p`.`is_active` AS `IsProductAvailable`
FROM `wishlist` AS `w`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `w`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `w`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
INNER JOIN `sku_product_variants` AS `s` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `s`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p0`.`file_name`, `p0`.`sku_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p0`
    WHERE `p0`.`sort_order` = 1
) AS `p1` ON `w`.`sku_id` = `p1`.`sku_id`
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`product_id`
    FROM `product_images` AS `p2`
    WHERE (`p2`.`sort_order` = 1) AND `p2`.`sku_id` IS NULL
) AS `p3` ON `p`.`product_id` = `p3`.`product_id`
WHERE `w`.`shopper_reg_id` = @__shopperId_0
ORDER BY `w`.`wishlist_id` DESC
LIMIT @__p_2 OFFSET @__p_1

) AS `s1` ON `s0`.`product_id` = `s1`.`product_id`

SELECT `s`.`product_id`, `p`.`bus_reg_id`, `b`.`business_name`, `p`.`buscat_id`, `p`.`prod_subcat_id`, `p`.`product_type_id`, `p0`.`prod_type_name`, `p`.`fabric_id`, `f`.`fabric_name`, `p`.`design_id`, `d`.`design_name`, `p`.`product_name`, `p`.`product_description`, `s`.`sku_id`, `s`.`color`, `s`.`size_id`, `p1`.`size_name`, `s`.`sku_cost`, `s`.`discount_price`, `s`.`quantity`, `s`.`length`, `s`.`width`, `s`.`height`, `s`.`weight`, `s`.`discount`, `p`.`product_id`, `b`.`bus_reg_id`, `p0`.`prod_type_id`, `f`.`fabric_id`, `d`.`design_id`, `p1`.`size_id`, `p2`.`file_name`, `p2`.`sort_order`, `p2`.`image_id`
FROM `sku_product_variants` AS `s`
INNER JOIN `products` AS `p` ON `s`.`product_id` = `p`.`product_id`
INNER JOIN `business_registers` AS `b` ON `p`.`bus_reg_id` = `b`.`bus_reg_id`
LEFT JOIN `product_type` AS `p0` ON `p`.`product_type_id` = `p0`.`prod_type_id`
LEFT JOIN `fabrics` AS `f` ON `p`.`fabric_id` = `f`.`fabric_id`
LEFT JOIN `designs` AS `d` ON `p`.`design_id` = `d`.`design_id`
LEFT JOIN `product_sizes` AS `p1` ON `s`.`size_id` = `p1`.`size_id`
LEFT JOIN `product_images` AS `p2` ON `s`.`sku_id` = `p2`.`sku_id`
WHERE ((`s`.`discount` IS NOT NULL AND (`s`.`discount` > 0.0)) AND (`p`.`product_status` = 'Approved')) AND `p`.`is_active`
ORDER BY `s`.`sku_id`, `p`.`product_id`, `b`.`bus_reg_id`, `p0`.`prod_type_id`, `f`.`fabric_id`, `d`.`design_id`, `p1`.`size_id`, `p2`.`sort_order`
